令u=exp(-lnr)v，v满足一维波动方程，得解</p><h3><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_1(t,r)=\frac{1}{r}[f(r-at)+g(r+at)]\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore =\frac{1}{2r}[(r+at)\phi(r+at)-(at-r)\phi(at-r)]+\frac{1}{2ar}\int_{at-r}^{r+at}\xi\psi(\xi)d\xi,t&gt;\frac{r}{a}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore target="_blank">二阶线性偏微分方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>分类/解法</h3><p data-pid="llFG6izC"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="a_{11}\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x^2}+2a_{12}\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x\partial y}+a_{22}\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial y^2}+b_1\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}+b_2\frac{\partial u}{\partial y}+cu=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">a_{11}\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x^2}+2a_{12}\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x\partial y}+a_{22}\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial y^2}+b_1\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}+b_2\frac{\partial u}{\partial y}+cu=0\\</span></span></span>1.解法</p><p data-pid="xgo3QNlw">有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" \Delta=a_{12}^2-a_{11}a_{22}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> \Delta=a_{12}^2-a_{11}a_{22}</span></span></span> <b>（注意如果是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_{xy}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_{xy}</span></span></span> 的系数的话，应该是系数^2/4)</b></p><p data-pid="QIfqdQKh"><b>突然发现用</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="b^2-4ac"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">b^2-4ac</span></span></span> 好像是一样的效果啊。。。</p><p data-pid="JEkvr2ur"><span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%89%B9%E5%BE%81%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">特征方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>中 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_{xx}\to (dy)^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_{xx}\to (dy)^2</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" u_{xy}\to dxdy"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> u_{xy}\to dxdy</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" u_{yy}\to (dx)^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> u_{yy}\to (dx)^2</span></span></span> 等等</p><p data-pid="9qbjcZaa">找出<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%89%B9%E5%BE%81%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">特征量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\xi \eta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\xi \eta</span></span></span>，带回<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%8E%9F%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">原方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>，求解。</p><p data-pid="i9_ZAQXg">2.分类</p><p data-pid="OLbzvBlj"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta&gt;0  \ 双曲 \ \Delta=0 \  抛物 \ \Delta&lt;0 \  椭圆"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta&gt;0  \ 双曲 \ \Delta=0 \  抛物 \ \Delta&lt;0 \  椭圆</span></span></span> </p><h3>6.叠加&amp;<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%BD%90%E6%AC%A1%E5%8C%96&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">齐次化<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="0p-RLq3p">叠加：找特解，然后把对原方程影响扣除。</p><p data-pid="lQDmtJzP">齐次化：冲量原理</p><p data-pid="rlP_ifPL">例： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" \frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial t^2}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}+f(t,x)\quad t&gt;0,\ -\infty&lt;x&lt;+\infty \\ u|_{t=0}=0,u_t|_{t=0}=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> \frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial t^2}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}+f(t,x)\quad t&gt;0,\ -\infty&lt;x&lt;+\infty \\ u|_{t=0}=0,u_t|_{t=0}=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="DLXQeckU">令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" u(t,x)=\int_0^t w(t,x;\tau) d\tau"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> u(t,x)=\int_0^t w(t,x;\tau) d\tau</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="t&lt;\tau时 \quad w=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">t&lt;\tau时 \quad w=0</span></span></span> ，方程变为</p><p data-pid="2M2uKEAT"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex=" \frac{\partial^2w}{\partial t^2}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 w}{\partial x^2}\quad t&gt;\tau,\ -\infty&lt;x&lt;+\infty \\ w|_{t=\tau}=0,\quad w_t|_{t=0}=f(\tau,x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder"> \frac{\partial^2w}{\partial t^2}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 w}{\partial x^2}\quad t&gt;\tau,\ -\infty&lt;x&lt;+\infty \\ w|_{t=\tau}=0,\quad w_t|_{t=0}=f(\tau,x)\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="R2Ha_-Ai">更高阶情况：<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=n-1%E9%98%B6%E5%AF%BC%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">n-1阶导数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>均为0，<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=n%E9%98%B6%E5%AF%BC%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">n阶导数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>为f</p><p data-pid="El2lzsTh">注意：在 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c(x,y,u)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c(x,y,u)=0</span></span></span> 的情况下，可以直接把u设成 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(\xi)+g(\eta)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(\xi)+g(\eta)</span></span></span> ，然后计算就行了</p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter  \ \ 2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter  \ \ 2</span></span></span>   <span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%88%86%E7%A6%BB%E5%8F%98%E9%87%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">分离变量<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h2><h3>1.弦振动</h3><p data-pid="SQAJAG_S"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial t^2}=a^2\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x^2}，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u|_{x=0}=u|_{x=l}=0\\ u|_{t=0}=\phi(x)，u_t|_{t=0}=\psi(x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial t^2}=a^2\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x^2}，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u|_{x=0}=u|_{x=l}=0\\ u|_{t=0}=\phi(x)，u_t|_{t=0}=\psi(x)\\</span></span></span> 用分离变量求解即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=TX"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=TX</span></span></span> ,得到</p><p data-pid="w04uiFst"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{T''}{a^2T}=\frac{X''}{X}=-\lambda\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{T''}{a^2T}=\frac{X''}{X}=-\lambda\\</span></span></span> 有非零解时 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda&gt;0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda&gt;0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="X=A\cos\omega x+B\sin \omega x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">X=A\cos\omega x+B\sin \omega x</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xa1i05Uc">由<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=2&amp;q=%E8%BE%B9%E7%95%8C%E6%9D%A1%E4%BB%B6&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">边界条件<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(0)=u(l)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(0)=u(l)=0</span></span></span> ，有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=(\frac{n\pi}{l})^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=(\frac{n\pi}{l})^2</span></span></span> ，带回T即可得到解</p><p data-pid="eqi41XIu"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\Sigma_{n=1}^{+\infty}(C_n\cos\frac{an\pi}{l}t+D_n\sin\frac{an\pi}{l}t) sin\frac{n\pi}{l}x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\Sigma_{n=1}^{+\infty}(C_n\cos\frac{an\pi}{l}t+D_n\sin\frac{an\pi}{l}t) sin\frac{n\pi}{l}x</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="3SJf4Iw7">代入初值条件，三角函数<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%82%85%E9%87%8C%E5%8F%B6%E5%B1%95%E5%BC%80&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">傅里叶展开<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>即可（两边同乘对应阶的三角函数）</p><h3>2.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E4%BA%8C%E7%BB%B4%E6%8B%89%E6%99%AE%E6%8B%89%E6%96%AF%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">二维拉普拉斯方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="nltBWemK"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x^2}+\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial y^2}=0，x^2+y^2=a^2\\ u|_{x^2+y^2=a^2}=F(x,y)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial x^2}+\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial y^2}=0，x^2+y^2=a^2\\ u|_{x^2+y^2=a^2}=F(x,y)\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="qcBvEtLJ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{r}\frac{\partial}{\partial r}(r\frac{\partial u}{\partial r})+\frac{1}{r^2}\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial\theta^2}=0，r&lt;a\\ u|_{r=a}=F(a\cos\theta,b\sin\theta)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{r}\frac{\partial}{\partial r}(r\frac{\partial u}{\partial r})+\frac{1}{r^2}\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial\theta^2}=0，r&lt;a\\ u|_{r=a}=F(a\cos\theta,b\sin\theta)\\</span></span></span> 即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Theta''+\lambda\Theta=0\\ \frac{1}{r}(rR')'-\frac{\lambda}{r^2}R=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Theta''+\lambda\Theta=0\\ \frac{1}{r}(rR')'-\frac{\lambda}{r^2}R=0\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="iGITrzB7"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\theta</span></span></span> 有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="2\pi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">2\pi</span></span></span> 周期性，得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=n^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=n^2</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Theta=A_n\cos n\theta+B_n\sin n\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Theta=A_n\cos n\theta+B_n\sin n\theta</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="LybGch0M">得 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r^2R''+rR'-n^2R=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r^2R''+rR'-n^2R=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="o3ReRAFr"><b>技巧：</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r=e^t"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r=e^t</span></span></span> 代换，得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R_0=C_0+D_0\ln r，R_n=C_nr^n+D_nr^{-n}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R_0=C_0+D_0\ln r，R_n=C_nr^n+D_nr^{-n}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lK8KADtN">仍然是三角函数傅里叶展开，这里有一个Poisson积分公式</p><p data-pid="K-qDP6Y5"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi}f(\phi)[1+2\Sigma_{n=1}^\infty (\frac{r}{a})^n\cos(n(\phi-\theta))]d\phi，r&lt;a\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi}f(\phi)[1+2\Sigma_{n=1}^\infty (\frac{r}{a})^n\cos(n(\phi-\theta))]d\phi，r&lt;a\\</span></span></span> 要使用 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\cos x=Re(e^{ix})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\cos x=Re(e^{ix})</span></span></span> 化简式子，即</p><p data-pid="GPBo7xLa"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Sigma_{n=1}^\infty (\frac{r}{a})^n\cos(n(\phi-\theta))=\frac{ar\cos(\phi-\theta)-r^2}{a^2+r^2-2ar\cos\theta}，r&lt;a\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Sigma_{n=1}^\infty (\frac{r}{a})^n\cos(n(\phi-\theta))=\frac{ar\cos(\phi-\theta)-r^2}{a^2+r^2-2ar\cos\theta}，r&lt;a\\</span></span></span> 所以 得到poisson积分公式<span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{a^2-r^2}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi}\frac{f(\phi)}{a^2+r^2-2ar\cos(\phi-\theta)}d\phi，r&lt;a\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{a^2-r^2}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi}\frac{f(\phi)}{a^2+r^2-2ar\cos(\phi-\theta)}d\phi，r&lt;a\\</span></span></span> 或者采用原级数解</p><h3><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{A_0}{2}+\Sigma_{n=1}^{+\infty}(\frac{a}{r})^n(A_n\cos n\theta+B_n\sin n\theta)\quad (r&gt;a)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{A_0}{2}+\Sigma_{n=1}^{+\infty}(\frac{a}{r})^n(A_n\cos n\theta+B_n\sin n\theta)\quad (r&gt;a)\\</span></span></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{C_0}{2}+\frac{D_0}{2}\ln r+\Sigma_{n=1}^{+\infty}((C_nr^n+D_nr^{-n})(A_n\cos n\theta+B_n\sin n\theta)\quad (a_1&gt;r&gt;a_2)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{C_0}{2}+\frac{D_0}{2}\ln r+\Sigma_{n=1}^{+\infty}((C_nr^n+D_nr^{-n})(A_n\cos n\theta+B_n\sin n\theta)\quad (a_1&gt;r&gt;a_2)\\</span></span></span> 3.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=S-L%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%E5%9E%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">S-L标准型<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="9GJ-ppiC"><b>标准形式 </b><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="[k(x)X'(x)]'-q(x)X(x)+\lambda\rho(x)X(x)=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">[k(x)X'(x)]'-q(x)X(x)+\lambda\rho(x)X(x)=0\\</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="c_j=\frac{\int_a^bf(x)X_j(x)dx}{\int_a^b|X_j(x)|^2dx}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">c_j=\frac{\int_a^bf(x)X_j(x)dx}{\int_a^b|X_j(x)|^2dx}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(x)=\Sigma_{j=1}^{+\infty}c_jX_j(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(x)=\Sigma_{j=1}^{+\infty}c_jX_j(x)</span></span></span>     注意使用条件确定固有值 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda</span></span></span> </p><h3>4.非<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E9%BD%90%E6%AC%A1%E9%97%AE%E9%A2%98&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">齐次问题<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="sJCcUM9P"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}=f(t,x)，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u|_{x=0}=u|_{x=l}=0\\ u|_{t=0}=\phi(x)，u_t|_{t=0}=\psi(x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2u}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}=f(t,x)，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u|_{x=0}=u|_{x=l}=0\\ u|_{t=0}=\phi(x)，u_t|_{t=0}=\psi(x)\\</span></span></span><b>1.特解法：即找到特殊函数使得</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2v}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 v}{\partial x^2}=f(t,x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2v}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 v}{\partial x^2}=f(t,x)</span></span></span> ，再修改边界条件与初值条件，这样可转化为达朗贝尔函数求解问题</p><p data-pid="HKM6OQiN"><b>2.冲量原理法：</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=u_1+u_2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=u_1+u_2</span></span></span> ，先使得边界条件满足（把初值和边界条件都抹零）即</p><p data-pid="adjRVpD0"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2u_2}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 u_2}{\partial x^2}=0，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u_2|_{x=0}=u_2|_{x=l}=0\\ u_2|_{t=0}=\phi(x)，(u_2)_t|_{t=0}=\psi(x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2u_2}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 u_2}{\partial x^2}=0，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u_2|_{x=0}=u_2|_{x=l}=0\\ u_2|_{t=0}=\phi(x)，(u_2)_t|_{t=0}=\psi(x)\\</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2u_1}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 u_1}{\partial x^2}=f(t,x)，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u|_{x=0}=u|_{x=l}=0\\ u|_{t=0}=0，u_t|_{t=0}=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2u_1}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 u_1}{\partial x^2}=f(t,x)，t&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ u|_{x=0}=u|_{x=l}=0\\ u|_{t=0}=0，u_t|_{t=0}=0\\</span></span></span> 即： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial^2w}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 w}{\partial x^2}=，\tau&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ w|_{x=0}=w|_{x=l}=0\\ w|_{t=\tau}=\phi(x)，w_\tau|_{\tau=t}=f(\tau,x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial^2w}{\partial t^2}-a^2\frac{\partial^2 w}{\partial x^2}=，\tau&gt;0，0&lt;x&lt;l\\ w|_{x=0}=w|_{x=l}=0\\ w|_{t=\tau}=\phi(x)，w_\tau|_{\tau=t}=f(\tau,x)\\</span></span></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_1=\int_{0}^t w(\tau)d\tau\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_1=\int_{0}^t w(\tau)d\tau\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="Fj5dV05z"><b>3.Fourier展开法：先从齐次形式中找出<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%9B%BA%E6%9C%89%E5%87%BD%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">固有函数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>和固有值，再对非线性项，边界项，初始项做广义傅里叶展开</b></p><p data-pid="2lutLEfA"><b>一般情况：</b>可一起使用这三种</p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter  \ \ 3"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter  \ \ 3</span></span></span>   特殊函数(Legendre&amp;Bessel)</h2><h3>1.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=Bessel%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">Bessel方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>导出（柱）</h3><p data-pid="vpXg0HrL"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_3=\frac{1}{r}\frac{\partial }{\partial r}(r\frac{\partial }{\partial r})+\frac{1}{r^2}\frac{\partial^2}{\partial \theta^2}+\frac{\partial^2}{\partial z^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_3=\frac{1}{r}\frac{\partial }{\partial r}(r\frac{\partial }{\partial r})+\frac{1}{r^2}\frac{\partial^2}{\partial \theta^2}+\frac{\partial^2}{\partial z^2}</span></span></span> ，<b>（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%9F%B1%E5%9D%90%E6%A0%87&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">柱坐标<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</b>分离 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Z,\Theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Z,\Theta</span></span></span> ，得 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(rR')'+(\lambda r-\frac{\nu^2}{r})R=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(rR')'+(\lambda r-\frac{\nu^2}{r})R=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="UjlQy3zB"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^2y''+xy'+(x^2-\nu^2)y=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^2y''+xy'+(x^2-\nu^2)y=0</span></span></span> ，( <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=\sqrt{\lambda}r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=\sqrt{\lambda}r</span></span></span> ） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y=R(\frac{x}{\lambda})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y=R(\frac{x}{\lambda})</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.Legendre方程导出（球）</h3><p data-pid="d3G_tbqh"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_3=\frac{1}{r^2}\frac{\partial }{\partial r}(r^2\frac{\partial}{\partial r})+\frac{1}{r^2\sin\theta}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta}(\sin \theta\frac{\partial}{\partial \theta})+\frac{1}{r^2\sin^2\theta}\frac{\partial^2}{\partial\phi^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_3=\frac{1}{r^2}\frac{\partial }{\partial r}(r^2\frac{\partial}{\partial r})+\frac{1}{r^2\sin\theta}\frac{\partial}{\partial\theta}(\sin \theta\frac{\partial}{\partial \theta})+\frac{1}{r^2\sin^2\theta}\frac{\partial^2}{\partial\phi^2}</span></span></span> <b>（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%90%83%E5%9D%90%E6%A0%87&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">球坐标<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</b>，令解= <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R\Theta \Phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R\Theta \Phi</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Lm4DHcVp">得 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Phi</span></span></span> 固有值为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu</span></span></span> ，另有 </p><p data-pid="SL4c3vVi"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{\sin\theta}(\sin \theta\Theta ')+(\lambda-\frac{\mu}{\sin^2\theta})\Theta=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{\sin\theta}(\sin \theta\Theta ')+(\lambda-\frac{\mu}{\sin^2\theta})\Theta=0\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="J_PdyCvA"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{r^2}(r^2R')'+(k^2-\frac{\lambda}{r^2})R=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{r^2}(r^2R')'+(k^2-\frac{\lambda}{r^2})R=0\\</span></span></span>对于 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Theta</span></span></span> 的方程，当 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu=0时,即轴对称情况"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu=0时,即轴对称情况</span></span></span> 令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=\cos\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=\cos\theta</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y(x)=\Theta(\arccos x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y(x)=\Theta(\arccos x)</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu=m^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu=m^2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="UsadvlE3"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="[(1-x^2)y']'+\lambda y=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">[(1-x^2)y']'+\lambda y=0</span></span></span> ，（<b>Legendre方程</b>）</p><h3>3.Legendre性质</h3><p data-pid="KsjpxImC">1.奇偶性</p><p data-pid="mPSg092_"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(-x)=(-1)^nP_n(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(-x)=(-1)^nP_n(x)</span></span></span> ，</p><p data-pid="-Wh0G-MR"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(0)=0，n=2m+1\ge 1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(0)=0，n=2m+1\ge 1</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="QWRz8dU-"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(0)=\frac{(-1)^m(2m-1)!!}{(2m)!!}，n=2m\ge2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(0)=\frac{(-1)^m(2m-1)!!}{(2m)!!}，n=2m\ge2</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="DND1765n"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(0)=1，n=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(0)=1，n=0</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="nUexCkYt"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(1)=1，P_n(-1)=(-1)^n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(1)=1，P_n(-1)=(-1)^n</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zeERpvPT">手搓 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n</span></span></span> ： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{2^n n!}\frac{d^n}{dx^n}(x^2-1)^n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{2^n n!}\frac{d^n}{dx^n}(x^2-1)^n</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="5sCjXMH1"><b>递推公式！！！</b></p><p data-pid="MqpVAR_u"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(n+1)P_{n+1}(x)-(2n+1)xP_n(x)+nP_{n-1}(x)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(n+1)P_{n+1}(x)-(2n+1)xP_n(x)+nP_{n-1}(x)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Svoz_TlE"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="nP_n(x)-xP_n'(x)+P_{n-1}'(x)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">nP_n(x)-xP_n'(x)+P_{n-1}'(x)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="hR-8S3J9"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="nP_{n-1}(x)-P_{n}'(x)+xP_{n-1}'(x)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">nP_{n-1}(x)-P_{n}'(x)+xP_{n-1}'(x)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="I5doom2f"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_{n+1}'(x)-P_{n-1}(x)=(2n+1)P_n(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_{n+1}'(x)-P_{n-1}(x)=(2n+1)P_n(x)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="wXe73_GW"><b>广义傅里叶-<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=Legendre%E5%A4%9A%E9%A1%B9%E5%BC%8F&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">Legendre多项式<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>展开</b></p><p data-pid="-LYrjd5k"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(x)=\Sigma {C_n}P_n(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(x)=\Sigma {C_n}P_n(x)</span></span></span> 即有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="C_n=\frac{1}{||P_n(x)||^2}\int_{-1}^{1}f(x)P_n(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">C_n=\frac{1}{||P_n(x)||^2}\int_{-1}^{1}f(x)P_n(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="sxb1itds">其中 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="||P_n(x)||^2=\frac{2}{2n+1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">||P_n(x)||^2=\frac{2}{2n+1}</span></span></span> （<b>注意这里的系数2对应积分区域是(-1,1)</b>)</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3><b>4.伴随Legendre方程导出</b></h3><p data-pid="AHCZrqbj">和Legendre方程的区别为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu</span></span></span> ，此时有 <span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E4%BC%B4%E9%9A%8F%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">伴随方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{\sin\theta}(\sin \theta\Theta ')+(\lambda-\frac{m^2}{\sin^2\theta})\Theta=0\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{\sin\theta}(\sin \theta\Theta ')+(\lambda-\frac{m^2}{\sin^2\theta})\Theta=0\\</span></span></span> 即让 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu=m^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu=m^2</span></span></span> ，还是令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\cos \theta=x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\cos \theta=x</span></span></span> 此时<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B%E7%9A%84%E8%A7%A3&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">方程的解<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}u(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}u(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_2cEt_eG">求导可证， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(x)=(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}v^{(n)}(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(x)=(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}v^{(n)}(x)</span></span></span> 为伴随Legendre方程的解， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="v(x)为Legendre方程解"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">v(x)为Legendre方程解</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lPM81b21">而伴随方程的解系由两部分组成： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n^m，Q_n^m"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n^m，Q_n^m</span></span></span> 对应Legendre方程的两个部分 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n，Q_n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n，Q_n</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xFP7VYUL"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n^m(x)=(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{d^m}{dx^m}P_n(x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n^m(x)=(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{d^m}{dx^m}P_n(x)\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="_ADcDFZO"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Q_n^m(x)=(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{d^m}{dx^m}Q_n(x)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Q_n^m(x)=(1-x^2)^{\frac{m}{2}}\frac{d^m}{dx^m}Q_n(x)\\</span></span></span> 其中Q在 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\pm 1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\pm 1</span></span></span> 处发散故一般要舍去</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.伴随Legendre函数性质</h3><p data-pid="VCAtAjaE">伴随Legendre函数固有值 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=n(n+1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=n(n+1)</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="TJkyCX7-"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="l\ge n \ge m"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">l\ge n \ge m</span></span></span> 时  有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{-1}^1 P_n^m(x)P_l^m(x)dx=\frac{2}{2n+1}\frac{(n+m)!}{(n-m)!}\delta_{nl}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{-1}^1 P_n^m(x)P_l^m(x)dx=\frac{2}{2n+1}\frac{(n+m)!}{(n-m)!}\delta_{nl}</span></span></span> ，说明正交和膜长平方</p><p data-pid="Uv0eyxQy"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="||P_n^m(x)||^2=\frac{2}{2n+1} \frac{(n+m)!}{(n-m)!}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">||P_n^m(x)||^2=\frac{2}{2n+1} \frac{(n+m)!}{(n-m)!}</span></span></span> <b>（积分范围(-1,1))</b></p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>6.一般情况球面问题（Laplace)</h3><p data-pid="DtGQ8xY-">一般来说有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi</span></span></span> 的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="2\pi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">2\pi</span></span></span> 对称性，所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Phi</span></span></span> 的解系为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\cos m\phi与\sin m\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\cos m\phi与\sin m\phi</span></span></span> （固有值 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="m^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">m^2</span></span></span> ）</p><p data-pid="mGkwJrrU">由前一个讨论 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Theta=P_n^m(\cos\theta)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Theta=P_n^m(\cos\theta)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="pnoOJDVC">两者组成<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%90%83%E8%B0%90%E5%87%BD%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">球谐函数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Y_{nm}=\Theta\Phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Y_{nm}=\Theta\Phi</span></span></span> ，Y也构成<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%AD%A3%E4%BA%A4%E7%B3%BB&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">正交系<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></p><p data-pid="p4KI8s4P">有  <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_0^{2\pi} d\phi\int_0^{\pi}Y_{nm}^{i部分} Y_{n'm'}^{j部分}\sin \theta d\theta=N_{nm}^2\delta_{nn'}\delta_{mm'}\delta_{ij}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_0^{2\pi} d\phi\int_0^{\pi}Y_{nm}^{i部分} Y_{n'm'}^{j部分}\sin \theta d\theta=N_{nm}^2\delta_{nn'}\delta_{mm'}\delta_{ij}</span></span></span> ，</p><p data-pid="KzrhiAvp"><b>注意这里的系数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho=\sin\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho=\sin\theta</span></span></span> 所以在后面又乘上了 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sin\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sin\theta</span></span></span> 的系数！</b></p><p data-pid="QFtlMfn2">这里i，j部分=1 or 2，表示取 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\cos m\phi还是\sin m\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\cos m\phi还是\sin m\phi</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="i9wAn_pu">其中 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N^2_{nm}=\frac{2\pi}{2n+1}\frac{(n+m)!}{(n-m)!}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N^2_{nm}=\frac{2\pi}{2n+1}\frac{(n+m)!}{(n-m)!}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="m\ge 1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">m\ge 1</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="m=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">m=0</span></span></span> 时， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{n0}^2=\frac{4\pi}{2n+1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{n0}^2=\frac{4\pi}{2n+1}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="jcZiAad0">广义傅里叶展开： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(\theta,\phi)=\Sigma_{n=0}\Sigma_{m=0}^{m=n}(C_{nm}\cos m\phi+D_{nm}\sin m\phi)P_n^m(\cos \theta)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(\theta,\phi)=\Sigma_{n=0}\Sigma_{m=0}^{m=n}(C_{nm}\cos m\phi+D_{nm}\sin m\phi)P_n^m(\cos \theta)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="a6G1cBtc"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="C_{nm}=\frac{1}{N_{nm}^2}\int_0^{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}f(\theta,\phi)P_n^m(\cos\theta)\cos(m\phi)\sin\theta d\theta d\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">C_{nm}=\frac{1}{N_{nm}^2}\int_0^{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}f(\theta,\phi)P_n^m(\cos\theta)\cos(m\phi)\sin\theta d\theta d\phi</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Fr_QP5bZ"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D_{nm}=\frac{1}{N_{nm}^2}\int_0^{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}f(\theta,\phi)P_n^m(\cos\theta)\sin(m\phi)\sin\theta d\theta d\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D_{nm}=\frac{1}{N_{nm}^2}\int_0^{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}f(\theta,\phi)P_n^m(\cos\theta)\sin(m\phi)\sin\theta d\theta d\phi</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="k3xl849P">也得到了<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E4%B8%89%E7%BB%B4%E7%90%83&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">三维球<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>坐标解形式： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\Sigma_{n=0}\Sigma_{m=0}^{m=n}(A_nr^n+B_nr^{-n-1})f_{nm}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\Sigma_{n=0}\Sigma_{m=0}^{m=n}(A_nr^n+B_nr^{-n-1})f_{nm}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>7.Bessel函数性质</h3><p data-pid="qzzjiEuj"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(x^{\nu}J_\nu)'=x^{\nu}J_{\nu-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(x^{\nu}J_\nu)'=x^{\nu}J_{\nu-1}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="d08YSI0d"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(x^{-\nu}J_\nu)'=-x^{-\nu}J_{\nu+1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(x^{-\nu}J_\nu)'=-x^{-\nu}J_{\nu+1}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="hQtxxm0f"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_0'=-J_1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_0'=-J_1</span></span></span> ，</p><p data-pid="R_QYAzx1"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="2J_\nu'=J_{\nu-1}-J_{\nu+1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">2J_\nu'=J_{\nu-1}-J_{\nu+1}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xeNdYrVX"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="2\nu x^{-1}J_{\nu}=J_{\nu-1}+J_{\nu+1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">2\nu x^{-1}J_{\nu}=J_{\nu-1}+J_{\nu+1}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="VhfZNw9g"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_{\frac{1}{2}}(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\sin x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_{\frac{1}{2}}(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\sin x</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_{-\frac{1}{2}}(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\cos x"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_{-\frac{1}{2}}(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\cos x</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="S_WJ07zc"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(x)=(-1)^{n+1}J_{-(n+\frac{1}{2})}(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(x)=(-1)^{n+1}J_{-(n+\frac{1}{2})}(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="l-vcDyJ0"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{-(n+\frac{1}{2})}(x)=(-1)^{n}J_{(n+\frac{1}{2})}(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{-(n+\frac{1}{2})}(x)=(-1)^{n}J_{(n+\frac{1}{2})}(x)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="nOLZh3wP"><b>计算积分时：</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\frac{1}{x}\frac{d}{dx})(x^{\nu}J_\nu)=x^{\nu-1}J_{\nu-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\frac{1}{x}\frac{d}{dx})(x^{\nu}J_\nu)=x^{\nu-1}J_{\nu-1}</span></span></span> 与 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(\frac{1}{x}\frac{d}{dx})(x^{-\nu}J_{\nu})=-x^{-(\nu+1)}J_{\nu+1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(\frac{1}{x}\frac{d}{dx})(x^{-\nu}J_{\nu})=-x^{-(\nu+1)}J_{\nu+1}</span></span></span> <b>很常用</b></p><p data-pid="YD1qgk1c"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lim_{x\to 0^{+}}J_{\nu}(x)=1，\nu=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lim_{x\to 0^{+}}J_{\nu}(x)=1，\nu=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lim_{x\to 0^{+}}J_{\nu}(x)=0，\nu&gt;0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lim_{x\to 0^{+}}J_{\nu}(x)=0，\nu&gt;0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="aiOir8e-"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lim_{x\to 0^{+}}N_{\nu}(x)=-\infty"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lim_{x\to 0^{+}}N_{\nu}(x)=-\infty</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lim_{x\to +\infty}J_{\nu}(x)=0，\lim_{x\to +\infty} N_{\nu}(x)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lim_{x\to +\infty}J_{\nu}(x)=0，\lim_{x\to +\infty} N_{\nu}(x)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zUVsuA-1">远端近似</p><p data-pid="dJ5Dr0mG"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_\nu(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\cos(x-\frac{\nu x}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_\nu(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\cos(x-\frac{\nu x}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4})</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_juf7KZ8"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_\nu(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\sin(x-\frac{\nu x}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_\nu(x)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi x}}\sin(x-\frac{\nu x}{2}-\frac{\pi}{4})</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>8.求解Bessel方程</h3><p data-pid="6tofKlK8"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(rR')'+(\lambda r-\frac{\nu^2}{r})R=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(rR')'+(\lambda r-\frac{\nu^2}{r})R=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lJtUzVKE"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|R(0)|&lt;+ \infty"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|R(0)|&lt;+ \infty</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha R(a)+\beta R'(a)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha R(a)+\beta R'(a)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ONq4SVBM">令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=\omega r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=\omega r</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y(x)=R(\frac{x}{\omega})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y(x)=R(\frac{x}{\omega})</span></span></span> 即可把方程化成 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^2y''+xy'+(x^2-\nu^2)y=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^2y''+xy'+(x^2-\nu^2)y=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="bkjPeO0x"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R_{\nu}=CJ_{\nu}(\omega r)+DN_{\nu}(\omega r)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R_{\nu}=CJ_{\nu}(\omega r)+DN_{\nu}(\omega r)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="DdAU8o9l"><b>第二步</b>（一般是解题第一步）定参数</p><p data-pid="uFiQkepJ">边界条件： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha J_{\nu}(\omega a)+\beta \omega J'_\nu(\omega a)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha J_{\nu}(\omega a)+\beta \omega J'_\nu(\omega a)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="vjSfN7lC">1类： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\beta=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\beta=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n^2=\omega_{1n}^2，\omega_{1n}为J_{\nu}(\omega a)=0的第n个正根"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n^2=\omega_{1n}^2，\omega_{1n}为J_{\nu}(\omega a)=0的第n个正根</span></span></span> 固有函数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R_n(r)=J_{\nu}(\omega_{1n} r) "><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R_n(r)=J_{\nu}(\omega_{1n} r) </span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="u9jo-bkK">2类： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n^2=\omega_{2n}^2，\omega_{1n}为J'_{\nu}(\omega a)=0的第n个正根"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n^2=\omega_{2n}^2，\omega_{1n}为J'_{\nu}(\omega a)=0的第n个正根</span></span></span> 固有函数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R_n(r)=J_\nu(\omega_{2n}r)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R_n(r)=J_\nu(\omega_{2n}r)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="qwHWyAx4">（注意2类时会有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nu=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nu=0</span></span></span> ）</p><p data-pid="DHQhUnR0">3类： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha \beta !=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha \beta !=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=\omega_{3n}^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=\omega_{3n}^2</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\alpha J_{\nu}(\omega a)+\beta \omega J'_\nu(\omega a)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\alpha J_{\nu}(\omega a)+\beta \omega J'_\nu(\omega a)=0</span></span></span> 的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="第n个正根"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">第n个正根</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R_n(r)=J_\nu(\omega_{3n}r)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R_n(r)=J_\nu(\omega_{3n}r)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="8E8OhWW9"><b>注意：Bessel函数的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho=r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho=r</span></span></span> ，在求傅里叶展开的时候要乘上</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="UVvyPgyb"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(r)=\Sigma_{n=0 or n=1}C_nJ_\nu(\omega_{kn}r)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(r)=\Sigma_{n=0 or n=1}C_nJ_\nu(\omega_{kn}r)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="IhViov9j"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="C_n=\frac{1}{N_{\nu kn}^2}\int_0^a rf(r)J_{\nu}(\omega_{kn}r)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">C_n=\frac{1}{N_{\nu kn}^2}\int_0^a rf(r)J_{\nu}(\omega_{kn}r)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="EGbJqYdX">另有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{\nu 1n}^2=\frac{a^2}{2}J^2_{\nu+1}(\omega_{1n}a)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{\nu 1n}^2=\frac{a^2}{2}J^2_{\nu+1}(\omega_{1n}a)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="8-d_02dM"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{\nu 2n}^2=\frac{a^2-\frac{\nu^2}{\omega_{2n}^2}}{2}J^2_{\nu}(\omega_{2n}a)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{\nu 2n}^2=\frac{a^2-\frac{\nu^2}{\omega_{2n}^2}}{2}J^2_{\nu}(\omega_{2n}a)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="h6V8H2-e"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{\nu 3n}^2=\frac{a^2-\frac{\nu^2}{\omega_{3n}^2}+\frac{a^2\alpha^2}{\beta^2\omega_{3n}^2}}{2}J^2_{\nu}(\omega_{3n}a)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{\nu 3n}^2=\frac{a^2-\frac{\nu^2}{\omega_{3n}^2}+\frac{a^2\alpha^2}{\beta^2\omega_{3n}^2}}{2}J^2_{\nu}(\omega_{3n}a)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>9.圆柱区域里的求解</h3><p data-pid="pRJKUcY5"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Theta</span></span></span> 方面只能是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sin m\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sin m\theta</span></span></span> 和 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\cos m\theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\cos m\theta</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ukT2vXqr">固有值 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_{mn}=\omega_{mn}^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_{mn}=\omega_{mn}^2</span></span></span> （这里m只是对应三角函数的参数，不是边界条件的编号）</p><p data-pid="ZUuQZmLN">得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\Sigma_{m=0}\Sigma_{n=0} (C_{mn}\cos m\theta+D_{mn}\sin m\theta)J_m(\omega_{mn}r)e^{-a^2\omega^2_{mn}t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\Sigma_{m=0}\Sigma_{n=0} (C_{mn}\cos m\theta+D_{mn}\sin m\theta)J_m(\omega_{mn}r)e^{-a^2\omega^2_{mn}t}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="MF4gYvhj"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="C_{mn}=\frac{1}{||\cos m\theta||^2||J_m(\omega_{mn} r)||^2}\int_0^{r_0}\int_0^{2\pi}\Phi(r,\theta)\cos m\theta J_m(\omega_{mn}r)rd\theta dr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">C_{mn}=\frac{1}{||\cos m\theta||^2||J_m(\omega_{mn} r)||^2}\int_0^{r_0}\int_0^{2\pi}\Phi(r,\theta)\cos m\theta J_m(\omega_{mn}r)rd\theta dr</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="9IRztgTz"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="C_{mn}=\frac{\delta_m}{\pi r_0^2J^2_{m+1}(\omega_{mn}r_0)}\int_0^{r_0}\int_0^{2\pi}\Phi(r,\theta)\cos m\theta J_m(\omega_{mn}r)rd\theta dr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">C_{mn}=\frac{\delta_m}{\pi r_0^2J^2_{m+1}(\omega_{mn}r_0)}\int_0^{r_0}\int_0^{2\pi}\Phi(r,\theta)\cos m\theta J_m(\omega_{mn}r)rd\theta dr</span></span></span> ,  <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta_m=1, m=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta_m=1, m=0</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="=2,others"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">=2,others</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="G8RJvsQM"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="D_{mn}=\frac{2}{\pi r_0^2J^2_{m+1}(\omega_{mn}r_0)}\int_0^{r_0}\int_0^{2\pi}\Phi(r,\theta)\sin m\theta J_m(\omega_{mn}r)rd\theta dr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">D_{mn}=\frac{2}{\pi r_0^2J^2_{m+1}(\omega_{mn}r_0)}\int_0^{r_0}\int_0^{2\pi}\Phi(r,\theta)\sin m\theta J_m(\omega_{mn}r)rd\theta dr</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>10.虚Bessel方程</h3><p data-pid="00iX6_HF"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(rR')'-\lambda rR=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(rR')'-\lambda rR=0</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\to"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\to</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^2y''+xy'-x^2y=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^2y''+xy'-x^2y=0</span></span></span> <b>（最后一项符号变负了）</b></p><p data-pid="R7c5NPMV">此时令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="ix=\eta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">ix=\eta</span></span></span> 可换为Bessel解法</p><p data-pid="fEmyo71P"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y(x)=CJ_\nu(ix)+DN_\nu(ix)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y(x)=CJ_\nu(ix)+DN_\nu(ix)</span></span></span> ，也可以乘上e^{i...}让J，N变成<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%AE%9E%E6%95%B0%E5%87%BD%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">实数函数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>（感觉没必要了）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>11.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%90%83Bessel%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">球Bessel方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="2ytR2LQf">原方程 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_3 v+k^2 v=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_3 v+k^2 v=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="CvUIwTAv">首先 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="k=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">k=0</span></span></span> 时 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R=Cr^l+Dr^{-(l+1)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R=Cr^l+Dr^{-(l+1)}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="k\ne 0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">k\ne 0</span></span></span> 时令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=kr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=kr</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="z(x)=\sqrt{x}y(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">z(x)=\sqrt{x}y(x)</span></span></span> 得到</p><p data-pid="6LeS5fsX"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^2z''+xz'+[x^2-(l+\frac{1}{2})^2]z=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^2z''+xz'+[x^2-(l+\frac{1}{2})^2]z=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="XTCYUzOc"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="z(x)=CJ_{l+\frac{1}{2}}+DN_{l+\frac{1}{2}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">z(x)=CJ_{l+\frac{1}{2}}+DN_{l+\frac{1}{2}}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y(x)=(CJ_{l+\frac{1}{2}}+DN_{l+\frac{1}{2}})\frac{1}{\sqrt{x}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y(x)=(CJ_{l+\frac{1}{2}}+DN_{l+\frac{1}{2}})\frac{1}{\sqrt{x}}</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="=C'j_l(x)+D'n_l(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">=C'j_l(x)+D'n_l(x)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>12.解球Bessel方程</h3><p data-pid="qVYj32a9"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(r^2R')'+[k^2r^2-l(l+1)]R=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(r^2R')'+[k^2r^2-l(l+1)]R=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_ZD5AopC"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R(0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R(0)</span></span></span> 有界， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R(r_0)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R(r_0)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="WfJ2uwOL">即 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R(r)=j_l(kr)=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2kr}}J_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(kr)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R(r)=j_l(kr)=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2kr}}J_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(kr)</span></span></span> ，又因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R(r_0)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R(r_0)=0</span></span></span> 所以 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="k_n"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">k_n</span></span></span> 为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(kr)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(kr)</span></span></span> 的第n个正根</p><p data-pid="_G7F1RWh">固有函数： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2k_n r}}J_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(k_n r)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2k_n r}}J_{n+\frac{1}{2}}(k_n r)</span></span></span> ，对于 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_l"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_l</span></span></span> 的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\rho=r^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\rho=r^2</span></span></span> ，（平方x除一下就是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_l的\rho"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_l的\rho</span></span></span> )</p><p data-pid="ZhqlwbXr"><b>模平方：</b> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="N_{ln}^2=\int_0^{r_0}r^2j^2_l(k_n r)=\frac{r_0^3}{2}j_{l+1}^2(k_nr_0)dr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">N_{ln}^2=\int_0^{r_0}r^2j^2_l(k_n r)=\frac{r_0^3}{2}j_{l+1}^2(k_nr_0)dr</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="VFOleXaD"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(r)=\Sigma_{n=1}C_nj_l(k_nr)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(r)=\Sigma_{n=1}C_nj_l(k_nr)</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="C_n=\frac{1}{N^2_{ln}}\int_0^{r_0}r^2f(r)j_l(k_nr)dr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">C_n=\frac{1}{N^2_{ln}}\int_0^{r_0}r^2f(r)j_l(k_nr)dr</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="UJQwO3Qc">感觉这个 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j和J的关系"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j和J的关系</span></span></span> 还是能记一下的</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="q4JOe_2Z"><b>总结：</b></p><p data-pid="IiVuVzcY"><b>Step1: 观察是柱坐标还是球坐标</b></p><p data-pid="KWZdY5e3"><b>Step2: 若为柱坐标 解中含有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_{\nu}(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_{\nu}(x)</span></span></span>  注意<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%9D%83%E5%87%BD%E6%95%B0&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">权函数<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r</span></span></span> !</b></p><p data-pid="afVhUMvD"><b>若为球坐标 且有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi</span></span></span>  解中含有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n^m(\cos \theta)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n^m(\cos \theta)</span></span></span> ，权函数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sin \theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sin \theta</span></span></span> ！</b></p><p data-pid="ovdYqQZw"><b>无 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi</span></span></span> ，解含有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(\cos \theta)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(\cos \theta)</span></span></span>   权函数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\sin \theta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\sin \theta</span></span></span> ！</b></p><p data-pid="plaPZUdT"><b>注意 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n(x)</span></span></span> 是没有权函数的</b></p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="qbUoqS0S"><b>标准形式熟记：</b></p><p data-pid="sIuiFUr4"><b><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(rR')'+(\lambda r-\frac{\nu^2}{r})R=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(rR')'+(\lambda r-\frac{\nu^2}{r})R=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=\omega_n^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=\omega_n^2</span></span></span> (Bessel)， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nu</span></span></span> 对应 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="J_{\nu}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">J_{\nu}</span></span></span></b> </p><p data-pid="HTRiNVrn"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(r^2R')'+[k^2r^2-l(l+1)R]=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(r^2R')'+[k^2r^2-l(l+1)R]=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x=kr"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x=kr</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R=Cj_l(x)+Dn_l(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R=Cj_l(x)+Dn_l(x)</span></span></span> <b>(球Bessel)</b></p><p data-pid="4S33VdxE"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="j_n=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2 x}}J_{x+\frac{1}{2}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">j_n=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2 x}}J_{x+\frac{1}{2}}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="n_l(x)=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2x}}N_{l+\frac{1}{2}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">n_l(x)=\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2x}}N_{l+\frac{1}{2}}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="svTerN4g"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^2 y''+xy'-(x^2+\nu^2)y=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^2 y''+xy'-(x^2+\nu^2)y=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\nu对应J_{\nu}(ix),N_\nu(ix)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\nu对应J_{\nu}(ix),N_\nu(ix)</span></span></span> <b>(虚变量Bessel)</b></p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="SsR34Nx5"><b><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="((1-x^2)y')'+(\lambda-\frac{m^2}{1-x^2})y=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">((1-x^2)y')'+(\lambda-\frac{m^2}{1-x^2})y=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=n(n+1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=n(n+1)</span></span></span> (legendre)</b></p><p data-pid="3StHh94S"><b>换为三角： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{\sin \theta}(\sin \theta \Theta')'+(\lambda-\frac{\mu}{\sin^2 \theta})\Theta=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{\sin \theta}(\sin \theta \Theta')'+(\lambda-\frac{\mu}{\sin^2 \theta})\Theta=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu_m=m^2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu_m=m^2</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lambda_n=n(n+1)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lambda_n=n(n+1)</span></span></span></b> ，对应 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="P_n^m"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">P_n^m</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 4"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 4</span></span></span>   <span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%A7%AF%E5%88%86%E5%8F%98%E6%8D%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">积分变换<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>法</h2><h3>1.Fourier变换引入</h3><p data-pid="uoN_c6pY"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar{f}(\lambda)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}f(x)e^{-i\lambda x}dx"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar{f}(\lambda)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}f(x)e^{-i\lambda x}dx</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(x)=\frac{1}{2\pi} \bar{f}(\lambda)e^{i\lambda x}d\lambda"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(x)=\frac{1}{2\pi} \bar{f}(\lambda)e^{i\lambda x}d\lambda</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="BREmSos0">令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f(x)]=\bar{f}(\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f(x)]=\bar{f}(\lambda)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zUMmy-u5"><b>性质: 1.</b>线性系数可以直接提出，</p><p data-pid="KaM7SEfu">2. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f^{(n)}(x)]=(i\lambda)^nF[f(x)]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f^{(n)}(x)]=(i\lambda)^nF[f(x)]</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="YFg2xWDI">3. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f(x)]e^{i\lambda_0 x}=\bar{f}(\lambda-\lambda_0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f(x)]e^{i\lambda_0 x}=\bar{f}(\lambda-\lambda_0)</span></span></span> （好理解）， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f(x-x_0)]=\bar{f}(\lambda)e^{-ix_0\lambda}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f(x-x_0)]=\bar{f}(\lambda)e^{-ix_0\lambda}</span></span></span> (好算）</p><p data-pid="Oo55MMeX">4. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f(\alpha x)]=\frac{1}{|\alpha|}\bar{f}(\frac{\lambda}{\alpha})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f(\alpha x)]=\frac{1}{|\alpha|}\bar{f}(\frac{\lambda}{\alpha})</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="qh33sZzo">5. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[\int_{-\infty}^x f(\epsilon)d\epsilon]=\frac{1}{i\lambda}\bar{f}(\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[\int_{-\infty}^x f(\epsilon)d\epsilon]=\frac{1}{i\lambda}\bar{f}(\lambda)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="8bnXv8Zi">6.（卷积） <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(x)*g(x)=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}f(x-\epsilon)g(\epsilon)d\epsilon"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(x)*g(x)=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}f(x-\epsilon)g(\epsilon)d\epsilon</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="cn5ONkAC"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f(x)*g(x)]=\bar{f}(\lambda)\bar{g}(\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f(x)*g(x)]=\bar{f}(\lambda)\bar{g}(\lambda)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="iDQpUlEp">7. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[\bar{f}(x)]=2\pi f(-\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[\bar{f}(x)]=2\pi f(-\lambda)</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>2.Fourier法解方程</h3><p data-pid="OObEZ7n_"><b>1.无界情况</b></p><p data-pid="huiApNyl">e.g. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="_5wNDclH"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u|_{t=0}=\phi(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u|_{t=0}=\phi(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="nr_6lvZI">先左右傅里叶： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{d\bar u}{dt}=a^2(-\lambda^2)\bar u"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{d\bar u}{dt}=a^2(-\lambda^2)\bar u</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar u_{t=0}=\bar \phi"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar u_{t=0}=\bar \phi</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="mh6WnMyV">再解出 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar u=\bar \phi(\lambda)e^{-a^2 \lambda^2 t}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar u=\bar \phi(\lambda)e^{-a^2 \lambda^2 t}</span></span></span> ，做反变换 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(t,x)=F^{-1}[\bar \phi e^{-a^2 \lambda^2 t}]=\phi(x)*F^{-1}[e^{-a^2\lambda^2}t]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(t,x)=F^{-1}[\bar \phi e^{-a^2 \lambda^2 t}]=\phi(x)*F^{-1}[e^{-a^2\lambda^2}t]</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="KfWr7WC7">（ <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F^{-1}[]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F^{-1}[]</span></span></span> 积分时取实部，尽量会算，实在不行就列个式子（悲））</p><p data-pid="SzKPYh1u">最后按照卷积写一下就行</p><p data-pid="DOeAcFNK">常微分线性方程 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{dy}{dx}+p(x)y=q(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{dy}{dx}+p(x)y=q(x)</span></span></span> 通解</p><p data-pid="8vKkB9hH"><span
