class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y = e^{-\int p(x)dx} \left( \int q(x)e^{\int p(x)dx}dx + C \right) "><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y = e^{-\int p(x)dx} \left( \int q(x)e^{\int p(x)dx}dx + C \right) </span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="yXQgAU4o"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F^{-1}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F^{-1}</span></span></span> 可以直接作用到积分号里面</p><p data-pid="ttUXINun">尽量写e^{i..}形式（比如三角可以改成这个）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="NLhZHaKL"><b>2.半无界</b></p><p data-pid="4mJ72UJf">奇延拓： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar f(\lambda)=\int_0^{+\infty}f(x)\sin(\lambda x)dx"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar f(\lambda)=\int_0^{+\infty}f(x)\sin(\lambda x)dx</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="D1pGekqP">反变换： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(x)=\frac{2}{\pi}\int_0^{+\infty}\bar f(\lambda)\sin(\lambda x)dx"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(x)=\frac{2}{\pi}\int_0^{+\infty}\bar f(\lambda)\sin(\lambda x)dx</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xWulvHZE">偶延拓： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar f(\lambda)=\int_0^{+\infty}f(x)\cos(\lambda x)dx"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar f(\lambda)=\int_0^{+\infty}f(x)\cos(\lambda x)dx</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="XmUscMlw">反变换： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(x)=\frac{2}{\pi}\int_0^{+\infty}\bar f(\lambda)\cos(\lambda x)dx"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(x)=\frac{2}{\pi}\int_0^{+\infty}\bar f(\lambda)\cos(\lambda x)dx</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="wohuQs8I"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u|_{x=}=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u|_{x=}=0</span></span></span> 奇延拓， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_x|_x=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_x|_x=0</span></span></span> 偶延拓</p><p data-pid="xMO1GSm5">步骤：奇/偶延拓，解出 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar u"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar u</span></span></span> ，再反变换回去，按照定义求反变换</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.高维情况</h3><p data-pid="oMLykMSc">其实没啥区别，按照几个方向拆开，分别设置对应的变量 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\mu,\lambda,\nu"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\mu,\lambda,\nu</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{d}{dx}\to\frac{\partial  }{\partial x}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{d}{dx}\to\frac{\partial  }{\partial x}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="4fD7_FRx"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[\Delta_3f]=-(\lambda^2+\mu^2+\nu^2)(F[f])"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[\Delta_3f]=-(\lambda^2+\mu^2+\nu^2)(F[f])</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="5chqZ4TC">还是有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[f*g]=F[f]F[g]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[f*g]=F[f]F[g]</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="t5Zq_oRY">解法与低维一样</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.Laplace变换引入</h3><p data-pid="V_P0X1Vf">定义 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(t)=f(t)H(t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(t)=f(t)H(t)</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="H(t)=1，t\ge 0，H(t)=0，t&lt;0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">H(t)=1，t\ge 0，H(t)=0，t&lt;0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="4dDteWQw">令 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p=\sigma+i\lambda"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p=\sigma+i\lambda</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\tilde f(p)=\int_0^{+\infty}f(t)e^{-pt}dt"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\tilde f(p)=\int_0^{+\infty}f(t)e^{-pt}dt</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="I9M9lW4L"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(t)=L^{-1}[\tilde f(p)]=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\sigma-i\infty}^{\sigma+i\infty}\tilde f(p)e^{pt}dp"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(t)=L^{-1}[\tilde f(p)]=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\sigma-i\infty}^{\sigma+i\infty}\tilde f(p)e^{pt}dp</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="H6iq4Wo7">性质： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[\alpha f(t)+\beta g(t)]=\alpha L[f]+\beta L[g]"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[\alpha f(t)+\beta g(t)]=\alpha L[f]+\beta L[g]</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="AAu1seTW"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[f^{(n)}(x)]=p^{n}\tilde f(p)-\Sigma_{i=0}^{n-1}p^{i}f^{(n-1-i)}(0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[f^{(n)}(x)]=p^{n}\tilde f(p)-\Sigma_{i=0}^{n-1}p^{i}f^{(n-1-i)}(0)</span></span></span> ，这里 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f^{(0)}=f"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f^{(0)}=f</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="HYYknh9R"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[f(t-\tau)]=L[f(t-\tau)H(t-\tau)]=e^{-pt}\tilde f(p)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[f(t-\tau)]=L[f(t-\tau)H(t-\tau)]=e^{-pt}\tilde f(p)</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p \to i\lambda"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p \to i\lambda</span></span></span> 和Fourier变换一样</p><p data-pid="UOHeMud9"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[f(t)e^{p_0t}]=\tilde f(p-p_0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[f(t)e^{p_0t}]=\tilde f(p-p_0)</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p_0\to i\lambda"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p_0\to i\lambda</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Y75rMI_F"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[f(\alpha t)]=\frac{1}{\alpha}f(\frac{p}{\alpha})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[f(\alpha t)]=\frac{1}{\alpha}f(\frac{p}{\alpha})</span></span></span> 这和 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F</span></span></span> 完全一样</p><p data-pid="tHT7wKMY"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[\int_0^tf(\tau)d\tau]=\frac{\tilde f(p)}{p}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[\int_0^tf(\tau)d\tau]=\frac{\tilde f(p)}{p}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="lBd-2NZB"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[f(t)*g(t)]=\tilde f(p)\tilde g(p)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[f(t)*g(t)]=\tilde f(p)\tilde g(p)</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="f(t)*g(t)=\int_0^tf(t-\tau)g(\tau)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">f(t)*g(t)=\int_0^tf(t-\tau)g(\tau)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="Jx3LXigr"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[(-t)^n f(t)]=\tilde f^{(n)}(p)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[(-t)^n f(t)]=\tilde f^{(n)}(p)</span></span></span> (疑似很有用！）</p><p data-pid="Fwz8AtX2"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[\frac{f(t)}{t}]=\int_p^{+\infty} \tilde f(p)dp"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[\frac{f(t)}{t}]=\int_p^{+\infty} \tilde f(p)dp</span></span></span> (疑似很有用！）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.Laplace法解方程</h3><p data-pid="vQf6s1BZ">e.g. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="(t&gt;0,x&gt;0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">(t&gt;0,x&gt;0)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="HkDQFLmO"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(t,0)=f(t)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(t,0)=f(t)</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(0,x)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(0,x)=0</span></span></span> <b>（这是“抹去多余项的边界条件，并不实际使用，一定是t=0时u=0）</b></p><p data-pid="XDpjODQz">首先左右Laplace变换， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="p\tilde u=a^2\frac{d^2 \tilde u}{dx^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">p\tilde u=a^2\frac{d^2 \tilde u}{dx^2}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\tilde u|_{x=0}=\tilde f(p)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\tilde u|_{x=0}=\tilde f(p)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="rUrGipR5">解出 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\tilde u=Ce^{\frac{\sqrt{p}x}{a}}+De^{-\frac{\sqrt px}{a}}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\tilde u=Ce^{\frac{\sqrt{p}x}{a}}+De^{-\frac{\sqrt px}{a}}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="3Bj8Ls6q">C项发散，舍去， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\tilde u=\tilde f(p)e^{- \frac{\sqrt{p}}{a}x}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\tilde u=\tilde f(p)e^{- \frac{\sqrt{p}}{a}x}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="42Q7oqYx">再逆变换即可</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="2MNlFkhx"><b>若 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(0,x)!=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(0,x)!=0</span></span></span> ，即类似 </b><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}=a^2\frac{\partial^2 u}{\partial x^2}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(t,0)=0,u(0,x)=\phi(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(t,0)=0,u(0,x)=\phi(x)</span></span></span> ，</p><p data-pid="Qyp6CZJ8">这是奇延拓，用上面的正弦形式Fourier变换求出解 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u_2"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u_2</span></span></span> 即可</p><p data-pid="jd5mn7_5"><b>注意<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%95%99%E6%95%B0%E5%AE%9A%E7%90%86&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">留数定理<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>的使用</b></p><hr><h2><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Chapter \ \ 5"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Chapter \ \ 5</span></span></span> 基本解方法</h2><h3>1. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta</span></span></span> 函数引入</h3><p data-pid="G-U8gHKR">即满足 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta(x)=+\infty，x=0; =0，x!=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta(x)=+\infty，x=0; =0，x!=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\delta(x)dx=1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\delta(x)dx=1</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="zkL07oFe">又有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\delta(x-\epsilon)f(x)dx=f(\epsilon)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\delta(x-\epsilon)f(x)dx=f(\epsilon)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="qIZuiD9U"><b>性质：</b>1. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta(x-\epsilon)=\delta(\epsilon-x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta(x-\epsilon)=\delta(\epsilon-x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="-T0pYlTT">2. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="a(x)\delta(x-\epsilon)=a(\epsilon)\delta(x-\epsilon)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">a(x)\delta(x-\epsilon)=a(\epsilon)\delta(x-\epsilon)</span></span></span> <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x^n\delta(x)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x^n\delta(x)=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="XkGBrvJm">3. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta[u(x)]=\Sigma_{k=1}^N\frac{\delta(x-x_k)}{|u'(x_k)|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta[u(x)]=\Sigma_{k=1}^N\frac{\delta(x-x_k)}{|u'(x_k)|}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="x_k"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">x_k</span></span></span> 为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u(x)</span></span></span> 的零点</p><p data-pid="saSENn7t">得到： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta(ax)=\frac{\delta(x)}{|a|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta(ax)=\frac{\delta(x)}{|a|}</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|x|\delta(x^2)=\delta(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|x|\delta(x^2)=\delta(x)</span></span></span> 这个要用 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta(x^2)=\lim_{a\to 0}\delta(x^2-a^2)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta(x^2)=\lim_{a\to 0}\delta(x^2-a^2)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="M8a-EDa-">4. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\delta^{(n)}(x)\phi(x)dx=(-1)^n\phi(0)^{(n)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\delta^{(n)}(x)\phi(x)dx=(-1)^n\phi(0)^{(n)}</span></span></span> 使用<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%88%86%E9%83%A8%E7%A7%AF%E5%88%86&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">分部积分<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>即可证明</p><p data-pid="PH4U4g63">5. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_0^{x}\delta(x)dx=H(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_0^{x}\delta(x)dx=H(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="cVnwpnEX">6.按照定义可证明： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta(x)*f(x)=f(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta(x)*f(x)=f(x)</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta^{(n)}(x)*f(x)=f^{(n)}(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta^{(n)}(x)*f(x)=f^{(n)}(x)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kEhXWHig"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="L[fg]=(Lf)*g=f*Lg"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">L[fg]=(Lf)*g=f*Lg</span></span></span> ，L为<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%BA%BF%E6%80%A7%E5%8F%98%E6%8D%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">线性变换<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></p><p data-pid="5aHLys_F">7. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[\delta(x)]=1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[\delta(x)]=1</span></span></span> ，故 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="F[1]=2\pi \delta(\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">F[1]=2\pi \delta(\lambda)</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\cos(\lambda x)dx=2\pi\delta(\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\cos(\lambda x)dx=2\pi\delta(\lambda)</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}xe^{-i\lambda x}dx=2\pi i\delta'(\lambda)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}xe^{-i\lambda x}dx=2\pi i\delta'(\lambda)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="xOoz051o">fourier展开和正常函数一样。</p><p data-pid="W4yUbSME"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta</span></span></span> 序列： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\frac{\epsilon}{\pi (x^2+\epsilon^2)}, \quad\lim_{\epsilon\to0}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}t}e^{-\frac{x^2}{2t^2}},\quad \lim_{N\to+\infty}\frac{\sin Nx}{\pi x}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\frac{\epsilon}{\pi (x^2+\epsilon^2)}, \quad\lim_{\epsilon\to0}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}t}e^{-\frac{x^2}{2t^2}},\quad \lim_{N\to+\infty}\frac{\sin Nx}{\pi x}\\</span></span></span> </p><h3>2.高维 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta</span></span></span> </h3><p data-pid="p757Dg69"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta(x-x_0.y-y_0,z-z_0)=\frac{1}{|J|}\delta(a-a_0.b-b_0,c-c_0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta(x-x_0.y-y_0,z-z_0)=\frac{1}{|J|}\delta(a-a_0.b-b_0,c-c_0)</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|J|为\frac{\partial(a,b,c)}{\partial(x,y,z)}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|J|为\frac{\partial(a,b,c)}{\partial(x,y,z)}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="aoQKUB4h"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{\text{R}^3} \frac{\partial^{m}\delta(x,y,z)}{\partial x^{m_1}\partial y^{m_2}\partial z^{m_3}}\phi(x,y,z)=(-1)^m\frac{\partial^{m}\phi(0,0,0)}{\partial x^{m_1}\partial y^{m_2}\partial z^{m_3}}\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{\text{R}^3} \frac{\partial^{m}\delta(x,y,z)}{\partial x^{m_1}\partial y^{m_2}\partial z^{m_3}}\phi(x,y,z)=(-1)^m\frac{\partial^{m}\phi(0,0,0)}{\partial x^{m_1}\partial y^{m_2}\partial z^{m_3}}\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="9NoKvvFs">故有 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta\frac{1}{r}=-4\pi \delta(x,y,z)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta\frac{1}{r}=-4\pi \delta(x,y,z)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="MSBmr80R">卷积性质与低维一致，fourier部分我看不懂。</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>3.基本解（ <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="Lu=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">Lu=0</span></span></span> )</h3><p data-pid="dCfw4Ptg">做法：设原方程解为U，例如原方程 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y'+ay=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y'+ay=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="SyANjZ_A">记 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="U'+aU'=\delta(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">U'+aU'=\delta(x)</span></span></span> (一维)，n维用n维的 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta</span></span></span> ，<b>当x取值范围不含0时</b> <b><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\delta"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\delta</span></span></span> 变为0</b></p><p data-pid="iHSjxFNe">算出 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u表达式"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u表达式</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="y=U*f(x)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}U(x-\epsilon)f(\epsilon)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">y=U*f(x)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}U(x-\epsilon)f(\epsilon)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="BE3Io1y6"><b>算u表达式可以使用“<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%B8%B8%E5%BE%AE%E5%88%86%E6%96%B9%E7%A8%8B&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">常微分方程<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>通解”or“瞪眼法”or“Laplace/fourier变换法”</b></p><p data-pid="IDy7krI_"><b>两个Green定理：</b></p><p data-pid="jbEiFSED">1. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_{S}u\frac{\partial v}{\partial n}dS=\int_{V}(u\Delta vdV+\nabla u\cdot \nabla v dV)\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_{S}u\frac{\partial v}{\partial n}dS=\int_{V}(u\Delta vdV+\nabla u\cdot \nabla v dV)\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="0ReGLjXR">2. <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\int_S(u\frac{\partial v}{\partial n}-v\frac{\partial u}{\partial n})dS=\int_V(u\Delta v-v\Delta u)dV\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\int_S(u\frac{\partial v}{\partial n}-v\frac{\partial u}{\partial n})dS=\int_V(u\Delta v-v\Delta u)dV\\</span></span></span></p><p data-pid="XOXAr644">左右同时降一维也是对的</p><p data-pid="rDfqyQ3k">定出U的表达式后需要确定系数</p><p data-pid="k-fHK2e3"><b>要选取有奇异性部分的解，e.g. N_0(kr), 看着会发散的解</b></p><p data-pid="c1k71wVm">然后取一函数 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(x)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(x)</span></span></span> 在含原点的有界区域外都是0</p><p data-pid="nCiOgtta"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\phi(0)=\int_{R^n}\delta \phi()=\int_{R^n}LU\phi\\ \phi(0)=\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\int_{D-D_{\epsilon}}(UL\phi-\phi LU)\\ =\lim_{\epsilon\to0}\int_{\partial D}((U\frac{\partial \phi}{\partial n}-\phi\frac{\partial U}{\partial n})-\int_{\partial D_\epsilon}(-U\frac{\partial \phi}{\partial n}-\phi\frac{\partial U}{\partial n}))\\ =\lim_{\epsilon\to0}\int_{\partial D_\epsilon}(-U\frac{\partial \phi}{\partial n}+\phi\frac{\partial U}{\partial n})\\"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\phi(0)=\int_{R^n}\delta \phi()=\int_{R^n}LU\phi\\ \phi(0)=\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\int_{D-D_{\epsilon}}(UL\phi-\phi LU)\\ =\lim_{\epsilon\to0}\int_{\partial D}((U\frac{\partial \phi}{\partial n}-\phi\frac{\partial U}{\partial n})-\int_{\partial D_\epsilon}(-U\frac{\partial \phi}{\partial n}-\phi\frac{\partial U}{\partial n}))\\ =\lim_{\epsilon\to0}\int_{\partial D_\epsilon}(-U\frac{\partial \phi}{\partial n}+\phi\frac{\partial U}{\partial n})\\</span></span></span> 之后按照<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E4%B8%AD%E5%80%BC%E5%AE%9A%E7%90%86&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">中值定理<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>化简即可</p><p data-pid="mqAxgXg2">注意把待求方程与Bessel,Legendre方程形式比较（渐进形式考试应该会给吧）</p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>4.Green<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%87%BD%E6%95%B0%E6%B3%95&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">函数法<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></h3><p data-pid="f2mGYyh_">基本上就用到那几个特殊的基础解U</p><p data-pid="qftR95Tt">在这里直接省略推导了（</p><p data-pid="Z64_uCbN"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_2u+k^2u=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_2u+k^2u=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{4}N_0(kr)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{4}N_0(kr)</span></span></span> 这 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{1}{4}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{1}{4}</span></span></span> 就是上面定系数定出来的</p><p data-pid="Bv1nK-Au"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_2u=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_2u=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln \frac{1}{r}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln \frac{1}{r}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="yzCVfU5o"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_2u-k^2u=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_2u-k^2u=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{2\pi}K_0(kr)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{2\pi}K_0(kr)</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="kvNDatMb"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_3 u+k^2u=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_3 u+k^2u=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=-\frac{\cos kr}{4\pi r}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=-\frac{\cos kr}{4\pi r}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="YfZMnveI"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_3 u-k^2u=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_3 u-k^2u=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=-\frac{e^{-kr}}{4\pi r}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=-\frac{e^{-kr}}{4\pi r}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="ZpZvvcuF"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_3u=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_3u=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{4\pi r}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{4\pi r}</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><h3>5.Green函数求解法</h3><p data-pid="TA2T1tHN"><b>1.镜像法</b></p><p data-pid="lUlg5ANs">第一边值问题模板： <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta G=-\delta(M-M_0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta G=-\delta(M-M_0)</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="G_{\partial V}=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">G_{\partial V}=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="uuw_qugm">步骤1：写下原解 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="U"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">U</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="G=U+g"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">G=U+g</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta g(M;M_0)=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta g(M;M_0)=0</span></span></span> ， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="g|_{\partial V}=-U(M,M_0)|_{\partial V}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">g|_{\partial V}=-U(M,M_0)|_{\partial V}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="fZgurjzz">一般情况：直线：在对称点放 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\epsilon"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\epsilon</span></span></span> 。平面：在对称点放 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\epsilon"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\epsilon</span></span></span> 。圆：在 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R^2/\rho"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R^2/\rho</span></span></span> 处放 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="-\frac{R}{\rho}\epsilon"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">-\frac{R}{\rho}\epsilon</span></span></span> ，（<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E7%94%B5%E5%83%8F%E6%B3%95&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">电像法<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>）</p><p data-pid="fgJ-TbGT"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="g"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">g</span></span></span> 分别对应-U，-U，- <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="R/\rho"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">R/\rho</span></span></span> U(<b>但是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="r(M,M_0)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">r(M,M_0)</span></span></span> 记得换)</b></p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="MZekaY3I"><b>2.<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%85%B1%E5%BD%A2%E5%8F%98%E6%8D%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">共形变换<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></b></p><p data-pid="TjB7wEoG">中心点是找合适的<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E5%88%86%E5%BC%8F%E7%BA%BF%E6%80%A7%E5%8F%98%E6%8D%A2&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">分式线性变换<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span>w。</p><p data-pid="OjLqt1FJ">二维上 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_2 U=-\delta (x,y), r&lt;1\\ U|_{r=1}=0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_2 U=-\delta (x,y), r&lt;1\\ U|_{r=1}=0</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="y069r1m5">解为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln \frac{1}{|w(z)|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln \frac{1}{|w(z)|}</span></span></span> </p><p data-pid="3rGKxbDh">怎么找：找对称点！以及边界</p><p data-pid="n9Av_ns-">e.g. 上半区圆心点为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="z_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">z_0</span></span></span> 对称点 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar z_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar z_0</span></span></span> 在单位圆里也要对称， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="z_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">z_0</span></span></span> 为0 故 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\bar z_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\bar z_0</span></span></span> 为<span><a class="RichContent-EntityWord css-b7erz1" data-za-not-track-link="true" data-paste-text="true" href="https://zhida.zhihu.com/search?content_id=257498820&amp;content_type=Article&amp;match_order=1&amp;q=%E6%97%A0%E7%A9%B7%E8%BF%9C%E7%82%B9&amp;zhida_source=entity" target="_blank">无穷远点<svg width="10px" height="10px" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="ZDI ZDI--FourPointedStar16 css-1dvsrp" fill="currentColor"><path d="m5.068 9.267-3.08-.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0-.994l3.08-.77a2.289 2.289 0 0 0 1.665-1.665l.77-3.08a.512.512 0 0 1 .994 0l.77 3.08c.205.82.845 1.46 1.665 1.665l3.08.77a.512.512 0 0 1 0 .994l-3.08.77a2.29 2.29 0 0 0-1.665 1.665l-.77 3.08a.512.512 0 0 1-.994 0l-.77-3.08a2.289 2.289 0 0 0-1.665-1.665Z"></path></svg></a></span></p><p data-pid="eLFHKuw9"><span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w=k\frac{z-z_0}{z-\bar z_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w=k\frac{z-z_0}{z-\bar z_0}</span></span></span> ，再定k，找Im=0的线，映射为圆边界(R=1)，因为 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|\frac{z-z_0}{z-\bar z_0}|"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|\frac{z-z_0}{z-\bar z_0}|</span></span></span> 为1</p><p data-pid="Douf2Q2O">故只要把k模长也设为1即可</p><p data-pid="olpJw-vl">同理，一个圆心原点的R半径圆盘。 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="z_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">z_0</span></span></span> 映射为原点， <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{R^2}{\bar z_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{R^2}{\bar z_0}</span></span></span> 映射为无穷远点</p><p data-pid="zvPg9IfD">再把 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\frac{R}{\bar z_0}z_0"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\frac{R}{\bar z_0}z_0</span></span></span> 映射为R=1的点，得到 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w=k\frac{R(z-z_0)}{R^2-z\bar z_0}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w=k\frac{R(z-z_0)}{R^2-z\bar z_0}</span></span></span> , <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="|k|=1"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">|k|=1</span></span></span> </p><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="F5ARdQKH">虚部在e指数上是角度，可以注意考虑一下。</p><p data-pid="Ekvdp6Jp">也可以映射到1/4平面区域，两圆相交，把俩交点设成0，无穷。然后在区域边界随便取一个点定单位一即可。</p><figure data-size="normal"><div class="RichText-ConditionalImagePortal"><img src="https://pic3.zhimg.com/v2-b5976b14b83168114aeb2ff3c8d9ab38_1440w.jpg" data-size="normal" data-rawwidth="1371" data-rawheight="483" data-original-token="v2-231bde1c37a876c43b6aafec9fa80708" class="origin_image zh-lightbox-thumb" width="1371" data-original="https://pic3.zhimg.com/v2-b5976b14b83168114aeb2ff3c8d9ab38_r.jpg"></div><figcaption> 上 下 边界各找点</figcaption></figure><p data-pid="fwHTvZwV">这样按镜像法在另外3象限各有- - +，用 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln \frac{1}{|w(z)|}"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln \frac{1}{|w(z)|}</span></span></span> 叠加（ <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w(z)"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w(z)</span></span></span> 换成 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="w(z,z_0{...})"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">w(z,z_0{...})</span></span></span> ）</p><p data-pid="y5JpNtsq">大概思路都可以像下面这个一样</p><figure data-size="normal"><div class="RichText-ConditionalImagePortal"><img src="https://pic2.zhimg.com/v2-cf1696622e222018aaac15cbdb76733f_1440w.jpg" data-caption="" data-size="normal" data-rawwidth="915" data-rawheight="307" data-original-token="v2-b0fcc6675b11e352137be2adac2aaaaa" class="origin_image zh-lightbox-thumb" width="915" data-original="https://pic2.zhimg.com/v2-cf1696622e222018aaac15cbdb76733f_r.jpg"></div></figure><figure data-size="normal"><div class="RichText-ConditionalImagePortal"><img src="https://pica.zhimg.com/v2-02375e4520666359d32d98aad8522b4c_1440w.jpg" data-size="normal" data-rawwidth="938" data-rawheight="507" data-original-token="v2-4992611947c6eeaf0bb45cce013e740f" class="origin_image zh-lightbox-thumb" width="938" data-original="https://pica.zhimg.com/v2-02375e4520666359d32d98aad8522b4c_r.jpg"></div><figcaption>来自复变函数（史济怀）</figcaption></figure><p class="ztext-empty-paragraph"><br></p><p data-pid="BUrXYshb">3.傅里叶方法</p><p data-pid="I7Fdp2A-">还是 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="u=Cu+g"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">u=Cu+g</span></span></span> ，把边界条件都挪到g去解决</p><p data-pid="LklnQuV3">在解g的时候就是正常使用分离变量or积分变换的方法。</p><p data-pid="T9hrmzqB">注意类似 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_2G"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_2G</span></span></span> 要自动补成 <span class="ztext-math" data-eeimg="1" data-tex="\Delta_2 v+\lambda v"><span></span><span><span class="tex2jax_ignore math-holder">\Delta_2 v+\lambda v</span></span></span> ，（加入固有值）</p><p data-pid="rgzdJdFq">（大概只考到这吧）</p><hr><p data-pid="sfdexsTO">6.22：上来直接发5张草稿纸。计算量果然炸裂。尽力了</p><hr><p data-pid="mNC7na9X">6.24：助教也尽力了，查不了一点分出来。期待老师再奶一口（</p><hr><p data-pid="OwpZ9SNr">6.25 光速出分 总评86 我、助教，老师都尽力了（）</p>
